# Gap table: frog

Made by `tools/views/gaps.py`. The 1200 species most recorded worldwide that borrow this view cover 98.0% of its 1,318,661 research-grade records; the 20 most recorded in Alachua County cover 100.0% of the county's 4,857. Counts are iNaturalist research-grade records through 2024. The view's own measured animal is not excluded: it shows as `m` where it was measured.

Row grades: `m` every trait of the row measured in that species; `g` every trait measured or carried by a rule; `i` at least one trait neither. The detail columns give the state of each trait.

Rows and their traits:

- **Movement**: motion
- **Speed**: cff_hz
- **Field of view, sharpness and pupil**: fov_h_deg, acuity_cpd, pupil_shape
